營收走勢圖

研究員/蔡佾璋 EASONTSAI@uni-psg.com

儒鴻 （1476）9 月營收 35.1 億元，+6%MoM，+8.3%YoY，在船期持續緊
張，仍有 4.5 億營收遞延至 10 月情況下，9 月仍創單月歷史新高紀
錄，淡季不淡，3Q26 季營收達成率 102.5%，符合預期，故研究部持
續推薦儒鴻（1476），主要考量:（1）產能滿載，目前訂單能見度達
2027 年3-4 月，3Q26 GM 將優於2Q26，4Q26 ASP 優於 3Q26，公司於
紡織展期間上修 4Q26 營收為季增，2026 年營收將明確呈現季季高，
對 4Q26 至 1H27 展望樂觀。（2）公司以高強度研發能量將中國同業
殺價競爭擋在防火牆外，開發動能維持高檔，具競爭力的高毛利新品
佔比達 15-20%。（3）Wellness 新趨勢需求強勢崛起，Wellness 重視
親膚性與多場景穿著功能，有利於儒鴻產品定位，儒鴻視 Wellness
為台灣紡織業的新機會，公司產品開發方向緊扣此趨勢，營收佔比已
從 2025 年的 20%提升至 1H26 的 30%左右，成長趨勢明確。 （4）依目
前 EPS 推算，2027 年發放之現金股利殖利率約 5.2%，具下檔保護。
研究部預估儒鴻 2026 年、2027 年稅後 EPS 24.38 元(原估 24.09 元)、
26.12 元(原估 25.33 元)， 分別+21.3%YoY、+7.1%YoY。 美伊戰爭讓市
場對於 2H26 消費需求擔憂延續，然儒鴻目前本益比僅約 12-13 倍，
位在過往評價區間 12-33 倍最下緣，股價位階偏低，下檔風險有限。
考量 2027 年獲利成長幅度雖然較低，但公司近期上修展望，故將本
益比調升至 14 倍，目標價 365 元（14xPER*2027EPS）。

重點摘要： 
1. 儒鴻為垂直整合成衣大廠  
儒鴻為布料織造、染整加工到成衣生產的垂直整合紡織廠，
主要產品為功能性針織布料與成衣。儒鴻本身布料的研發能
力強，對新、 舊客戶來說都能提供最符合潮流 、主流產品， 核
心競爭能力強，公司持續致力成為該領域的市場領先者。

針織布產線共四座廠房，分別為台灣的溪州廠、大山廠及大
園廠，產能合計為800萬碼/月；另有越南廠，產能為700萬碼
/月，總產能合計共1500萬碼/月。成衣自有產線主要位於越
南 、 印尼及柬埔寨 ， 平均產能為840萬件/月 ， 加上外包的策略
聯盟生產基地約505萬件/月，總產能達1,345萬件/月。

儒鴻最主要的客戶為Nike，其2025年營收佔比為16.4%，UA佔
比約9.1%，Lululemon佔8.9%，以1H26來看，前三大及前五大
客戶訂單加總呈現年增，具成長潛力的小巨人們目前合計占
比約10%，全年目標小巨人營收占比可達12-18%。

### Page 2

請參見末頁重要批露及免責聲明  2

儒鴻的成衣客戶有50個以上，布料客戶有150-200個，客戶充
分分散，公司持續培養小客戶，螞蟻雄兵持續貢獻營收 。

2. 儒鴻布料內轉比例提高，推動成衣營收持續增長 
儒鴻2026 年前3 季 成 衣 及 針 織 布 營 收 比 重 分 別 為69.1% 及
30.9%，成衣佔比較去年同期+3.5pctYoY。

成衣方面，2026年前3季營收209.1億元，+11.1%YoY。3Q26營
收70.9億元，-0.2%QoQ，+16.8%YoY。2Q26大量內轉的針織布
進行成衣加工後 ， 於3Q26反映在成衣營收上 ，3Q26的7月、9月
成衣營收皆創下歷史同期新高表現。新品牌客戶開發順利，
新客戶貢獻年增雙位數，既有客戶亦有個位數穩定成長。

### Page 3

請參見末頁重要批露及免責聲明  3

針織布方面，2026年前3季營收93.7億元，-4.6%YoY。3Q26營
收34.8億元 ，+16.8%MoM，YoY持平 。 針織布累積營收呈現微幅
下降，主因是公司貫徹垂直整合戰略，策略性提高自家針織
布供應給成衣部門使用的內轉比例主所致，針織布實際總生
產量與產能利用狀況並未衰退，仍維持年增表現。近五年針
織布外部銷售佔總營收比重自38%降至30%，布料內轉比例則
由25-30%大幅提升至40-50%。

3. 印尼三期擴廠計畫浮現 
印尼一 、 二期整體產能200~240萬件/月， 可貢獻總產值20億台
幣/年，滿編可達112條 ，目前產能全數開出， 稼動率滿載。 為
因應整體市場需求，公司規劃第三期工廠定位為高度自動化
廠區，未來若訂單持續成長並填滿新廠產能，高自動化所帶
來的邊際效益將會非常顯著。

新廠目前處於設計階段，產能規劃每月約200萬件上下。投產
初期會先以相對熟悉、 入門的產品進行試產。 訂單來源方面 ，
一部分會由越南廠轉單，一部分由品牌客戶直接下單。因規
劃導入更完善的自動化生產與管理系統，整體設計時程較原
先預期稍微延誤，管理團隊目前仍將量產目標設定在 4Q27開
始有實質產出。

由於印尼一、二廠目前產能滿載，公司正持續建置自有衛星
工廠以擴充產能，外包協力廠目前約占整體產能的27-28%。

### Page 4

請參見末頁重要批露及免責聲明  4

4. 預估儒鴻 3Q26稅後EPS  6.06元，+7%QoQ，+0.6%YoY 
儒鴻2Q26營收100.9億元，+4.8%QoQ，+3.8%YoY，為16個季度
以來新高，毛利率30%，-2.2pctQoQ，+4.4pctYoY，季減主因
為部分低毛利長青款產品客戶下單力道比公司預期更強，且
部分產品開始使用到較高價原料所致。費用率10.8%，略高於
預期，主因員工年度調薪所致，營業利益率降至19.2%，營業
利益19.3億元，-11.9%QoQ，+28.9%YoY，稅後EPS 5.66元，-
15.4%QoQ，+158.3%YoY。

儒鴻3Q26營收105.7億元，+4.8%QoQ，+9.7%YoY，3Q26達成率
102.5%，符合預期。在船期持續緊張，仍有4.5億營收遞延至
10月情況下，9月仍創單月歷史新高紀錄，淡季不淡。公司表
示產能滿載 ， 上修4Q26營收為季增 ，2026年營收將明確呈現季
季高，並對4Q26至1H27展望樂觀，研究部預估費用率維持穩
定，營業利益率 19.8% ，稅後EPS 6.06 元(原估5.91元)，
+7%QoQ，+0.6%YoY。

5. 預估儒鴻2026年、2027年稅後EPS 24.3 8、26.12元，分別為
+21.3%YoY、+7.1%YoY 
儒鴻目前產能滿載 ， 且需加班因應 ， 因此公司對2H26營運看法
為審慎樂觀 ，2026年單月營收皆可維持30億元以上 ， 毛利率維
持28-32%區間 。 研究部預估2026年營收409.9億元(原估404.7
億元)，+7.9%YoY，漲價後的原料於6、7月開始使用，但透過
長期合作與多元供應商策略，成本上漲幅度在可控範圍內，
預估全年毛利率為30.4%，+1.7pctYoY，營益率將+1.5ppts至
20.2%， 營業利益82.6億元(原估81.6億元)，+16.5%YoY， 稅後
EPS 24.38元(原估24.09元)，+21.3%YoY。

目前訂單能見度達2027年3-4月 ， 儒鴻對1H27營運看法仍維持
審慎樂觀 ， 研究部預估2027年營收453億元(原估441.7億元)，
+10.5%YoY， 毛利率29.8%，-0.6pctYoY。 全年營業利益88.1億
元(原估85.9億元)，+6.6%YoY， 本業營運持續提升 ， 稅後淨利
71.7億元(原估69.5億元)，+7.1%YoY，EPS 26.12 元(原估
25.33元)。

### Page 9

請參見末頁重要批露及免責聲明  9

附錄 一 ： 評 等 之 標 準 
評等  定義  
強力買進     預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬超過 25%以上  
買進  預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬 10% 以上  
中立  預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬介於 10～-10%  
降低持股  預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬介於 (-10%)以下  
未評等  沒有足夠的基本資料判斷該公司評等

### Page 1

ab 12 October 2026
Global Research
Nanya Technology
Further upside to expectations likely
Another strong quarter
Nanya Tech (NTC) reported strong 3Q26 revenue of NT$133.6bn (+62% QoQ,
+612% YoY) in line with UBSe of NT$132.7bn and 6% above Visible Alpha consensus
of NT$126.5bn. EPS came in at $25.40, slightly below UBSe of NT$26.44 and modestly
above consensus of NT$24.40. Growth was driven primarily by strong ASPs, up 65%
QoQ (vs UBSe of +50%) mainly on the back of the renewal of 6-month and 1-year

contracts that had expired, partially offset by lower-than-expected bit growth of -
5% QoQ (vs UBSe of +5% QoQ). Profitability, again, improved sequentially, with gross/
operating margins rising to 85.7%/80.0% from 79.5%/73.7% in 2Q26.
Fulfilment rates to remain low until Fab 5A ramp
NTC continues to see supportive supply-demand dynamics, with fulfilment rates for
demand outside of long-term contracts – which account for close to 70% of bits already

– remaining low. That  said, NTC sees limited upside to unit shipments in the near term,
given low inventory levels and limited floor space: (1) reiterating its full-year bit growth
guidance of "up high-teens % YoY," implying 4Q unit shipments will decline MSD
% QoQ (UBSe: -6%); and (2) guiding shipments to remain flat thereafter until its new
fab begins to ramp in 2H27. NTC's efforts to bring Fab 5A online are progressing well,
with equipment move-in scheduled for 1Q27 and initial production expected by 3Q27.

NTC continues to expect wafer capacity to reach 30k wpm by the end of 2028 and 50k
by the end of 2030. With demand remaining strong and existing capacity not enough to
fully address this, NTC continues to see upside to sequential ASP growth in the near
term, although at a more moderate pace.
Changes to forecasts
Reflecting 3Q26 results, as well as our new bit growth and ASP forecasts, we keep our
2026E forecasts largely unchanged but slightly raise our 2027E/2028E revenue forecasts

by 7%/5% to NT$655bn/NT$735bn (from NT$613bn/NT$702bn previously) and
EPS forecasts by 10%/7% to NT$128.16/NT$134.53 (from NT$116.41/NT$125.68
previously). Our 2027E/2028E EPS forecasts now sit 10%/8% above pre-results Visible
Alpha consensus.
Valuation: PT NT$640/share; maintain Buy
We value Nanya Tech shares at 2.63x NTM P/BV based on our 2028E-31E ROE estimate
of 21.4% and CoE of 8.1%.

### Page 2

Nanya Technology UBS ResearchNanya Tech (2408.TW)
Thesis Map     UBS                    Research     THESIS MAP a guide to our thinking and what´s where in this report
Q: Will DRAM undersupply continue into 2028?
Yes, in our view. Agentic AI is boosting further memory demand on multiple fronts beyond HBM,
including DDR5/LPDDR5 for conventional servers and CPU head nodes in AI servers. We now forecast
an acceleration of memory demand growth into 2027 with DRAM bit end-consumption growing

40% YoY vs 21% in 2026. As almost all of the incremental wafer capacity addition for DRAM is
going to HBM, supply is not catching up with demand. The main risk remains affordability by
customers as memory industry revenues are expected to reach US$1.69tn in 2027E.
Q: Can NTC grow as a niche DRAM player?
Yes, we think 2027 is likely to be a year of more constrained growth. Subsequently, though, we think
its process tech migration to 1c nm and 1d nm will likely lead to a 30% increase in bits per wafer for

each process tech migration (at comparable yields). NTC is also slightly pulling in its new fab ramp,
with equipment expected to move in 1Q27. We also see a tail of DDR3 and DDR4 products NTC can
address for a while.
Nanya Tech shares trade at 2.17x NTM P/BV, above +1SD of its 10-year average. That said, we believe
the strength of the unfolding DRAM cycle, as well as the tail of legacy products that Nanya can
address as a niche player, should continue to provide support for the stock.

Our industry checks suggest HBM demand continues to result in capacity allocation away from DDR,
which may moderate possible cyclical corrections, supporting upside to DDR contract pricing for
longer.
At 2.17x NTM P/BV, we believe Nanya Tech shares are discounting a LT average ROE of 17.6% vs our
LT estimate of 21.4%.

Company Description
Nanya Tech is a pure DRAM company based in Taiwan, focusing on legacy products for
consumers, broad-based applications and is now growing in servers.
Valuation Method and Risk Statement
Nanya Tech is a pure-play DRAM manufacturer based in Taiwan mainly focused on legacy DDR

products for customers across various end applications. We think downside risks to our thesis
could include a potential slowdown in end demand and hence the conventional memory
cycle as well as Nanya's legacy-centric product portfolio. We value Nanya Tech shares using a
target NTM P/BV multiple based on long-term average ROE and cost of equity.

KEY DEFINITIONS:Forecast Stock Return (FSR) is defined as expected percentage price appreciation plus gross dividend yield over
the next 12 months. In some cases, this yield may be based on accrued dividends. Market Return Assumption (MRA) is defined as
the one-year local market interest rate plus 5% (a proxy for, and not a forecast of, the equity risk premium). Under Review (UR)
Stocks may be flagged as UR by the analyst, indicating that the stock's price target and/or rating are subject to possible change in the

near term, usually in response to an event that may affect the investment case or valuation. Equity Price Targets have an investment
horizon of 12 months.

Investing in structured investments involves significant risks. For a detailed discussion of the risks involved in investing in any particular structured investment, you must read

the relevant offering materials for that investment. Structured investments are unsecured obligations of a particular issuer with returns linked to the performance of an
underlying asset. Depending on the terms of the investment, investors could lose all or a substantial portion of their investment based on the performance of the underlying
asset. Investors could also lose their entire investment if the issuer becomes insolvent. UBS does not guarantee in any way the obligations or the financial condition of any

issuer or the accuracy of any financial information provided by any issuer. Structured investments are not traditional investments and investing in a structured investment is
not equivalent to investing directly in the underlying asset. Structured investments may have limited or no liquidity, and investors should be prepared to hold their investment
to maturity. The return of structured investments may be limited by a maximum gain, participation rate or other feature. Structured investments may include call features and,

if a structured investment is called early, investors would not earn any further return and may not be able to reinvest in similar investments with similar terms. Structured
investments include costs and fees which are generally embedded in the price of the investment. The tax treatment of a structured investment may be complex and may differ
from a direct investment in the underlying asset. UBS and its employees do not provide tax advice. Investors should consult their own tax advisor about their own tax situation

before investing in any securities.

Betty.H.Chen@morganstanley.com                                                +852 2239-7213
Greater China Technology Hardware
Asia Pacific
Industry View In-Line
We keep our 3Q26 iPhone build estimate unchanged at 54M units (+4% q/q, -2%
y/y). But we have made adjustments to new iPhone 18 builds by lowering iPhone
Duo by 0.5M units to 0.5M, while raising iPhone 18 Pro Max by 0.5M units to 8.5M.

For 4Q, we lower our iPhone build estimate by 1M units to 72M units (+33% q/q,
-5% y/y), reflecting lower legacy iPhone models (iPhone 16/17e). Total new iPhone
18 model family units remains at 71M units (-21% y/y) in 2H26, with one less SKU
launched compared to last year. We continue to expect 7M units of iPhone Duo
builds in 2H26. With iPhone Duo sales starting soon, we will continue to monitor
ramp-up progress for any upside or downside to builds. Our revised full-year iPhone

build forecast implies CY26 shipment at 234M units (+3% y/y).
Our 3Q26 iPad build forecast is lowered by 0.5M units to 11.5M units (-12% q/q, -
18% y/y). We lowered 3Q entry level iPad builds due to  pull-forward demand in 1H
and the impact of rising prices. We also lower our 4Q iPad build forecast by 0.7M
units to 10.3M units (-10% q/q, -26% y/y). Our revised full-year iPad build forecast
implies CY26 shipments at 46.8M units (-14% y/y).

ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

Key Metrics

Company Sector 
Semiconductors

Company Description 
TSMC was founded in 1987 and is listed in Taiwan 
(2330TT) and the US (TSM/NYS).TSMC is the largest and 
global leader in integrated circuit (IC) manufacturing. As a 
build-to-order foundry, it provides a wide range of value-
add activities: IC manufacturing, mask-making, IC design 
services, turnkey solutions, and process development. We 
attribute its success to its proven, winning business model, 
unparalleled scale advantage, optimized execution, and 
technology scope and depth. 5 shares = 1 ADR.

Investment Rationale 
The ongoing semi content growth in mobile, rise of artificial 
intelligence (AI), and proliferation of Internet of Things (IoT) 
should result in sustainable upside in aggregate computing 
power globally. TSMC, as the leading contract manufacturer 
of semiconductor chips, is in a good position to capitalize. 
We are structurally positive on TSMC and expect it to 
sustain over 15% growth with rising FCF from 2026 
onwards.

### Page 4

Price objective basis & risk 
Taiwan Semiconductor Manufacturing Co. (TSMWF / TSM) 
Our PO for TSMC is NT$3,260 per share (US$620 per ADR), based on 20x 2027E P/E. 
TSMC's valuation has traded in the range of 10-26x PE from 2017. 20x is in the mid to 
high end of the historical range, above the average P/E of 16x. We think a PE above the 
average is justified given TSMC's enhanced industry position and mid-to-high 50% 5-
year CAGR in AI should underpin a rerating

Downside risks to our price objective are: (1) greater-than-expected slowdown in global 
smartphone/consumer electronics demand, (2) Intel's potential insourcing strategy and 
ambitions in foundry service, (3) execution risks on advanced nodes, and (4) higher than 
expected tariffs.

iQmethod are: A consistently structured, detailed, and transparent methodology. Guidelines to maximize the effectiveness of the comparative valuation process, and to identify some common pitfalls. 
iQdatabase ®  is our real-time global research database that is sourced directly from our equity analysts’ earnings models and includes forecasted as well as historical data for income statements, balance sheets, and cash

1 
6274 台燿 
(Taiwan Union Technology Co.)

報告日期： 2026/10/08 研 究 員：方姿婷  (Lesley Fang) 
壹、投 資 建 議 
重點摘要： 
 2Q26 營收符合預期， 毛利率受 1H26 漲價提升 ， 預期 3Q26 毛利
將順利反應成本上漲與優化之產品組合 ， 毛利率預估提升 3%+ 。  
 AI 伺服器帶動高階銅箔基板需求強勁，同時驅動高階網路交換
器升級動能，銅箔基板需要更高速傳輸、訊號低損耗之下會陸
續採用 CCL M8、M9 規格。 
 2H26 低軌 S 客戶 V3 版本新型火箭搭載低軌衛星，所採用之銅
箔基板升級至 M8 等級；以及，1.6T 光模組需求量增帶動銅箔
基板需求量增。

一. 2Q26 營收與財報符合市場預期，3Q26 營收優於預期  
台燿 2Q26 營收為 143.01 億元，+42.2%QoQ、+110.93%YoY，毛利
率為 29.72% ，稅後淨利為 23.42 億元，單季稅後 EPS+7.82 元。2Q26
台燿受到 ASIC CCL 專案出貨帶動，產品組合優化，毛利率已相較
1Q26 季增 4.5% 。台燿已公告 3Q26 營收為 179.48 億元(優於市場預
期 3%)，+25.50%QoQ，毛利率可順利轉嫁且高階 CCL 出貨比重提
升，預估台燿 3Q26 毛利率為 33.21%，稅後淨利 36.72 億元，單季
稅後 EPS+12.60 元。

二. AI 伺服器帶動 CCL/PCB 產值提升，產能擴充 33% 
宏遠投顧觀察，AI 伺服器與先進封裝等需求日增，高階 CCL 市場
需求將在 2024 年~2026 年 CAGR 達 26% ， 而高階 CCL 供給端 CAGR
則是 7%，高階市場需求殷切。高階銅箔基板(包含 AI 伺服器和高
階網路交換器，自 2024 年始採用 M8 CCL 材料；2026 年 M8 材料
佔總體 CCL 市場 40%，M8 材料佔比顯著高於 2022 年、2023 年的
22%和 26%。台系銅箔基板供應鏈當中，M6~M8 等高階材料的供
應廠商為(2383)台光電為首、(6274)台燿次之。回顧 3 年來，台燿
產能擴充進程，端視泰國廠進度而定。4Q26 泰國新廠產能陸續開
出，台燿總產能為 260 萬張/月，+13%YoY。展望 2027 年，台燿泰
國廠、常熟廠初步先提升 30 萬張/月，逐步提升總新增產能達 120
萬張/月，預估 2027 年底時，總產能達 380 萬張/月，+46%YoY；預
估 2028 年底時，台燿總產能擴充至 530 萬張/月，+39.5%YoY，平
均 3 年每年產能擴充約 33% 。高階 CCL 在 AI 伺服器、高速網路交
投資建議：買進

2 
換器(高階 Switch)帶動之下，一旦，泰國廠產能擴充可望使得台燿
市佔率得以提升 。 2027 年隨著 NVIDIA Rubin 平台將帶動 CCL M8+ 、
M9 材料出貨攀升，AI ASIC CCL 拉貨動能呈現 50~60%高度成長，
同業台光電與台燿高階 CCL 新產能釋出 ， 產品組合持續優化 。 2027
年起 ， 除一般型伺服器 Intel Diamond Rapids 平台 、 AMD Zen6 Venice
平台採用 PCIe Gen6 與 Ultra Low Loss CCL(M7)，PCB 板層數增至
20~24L；2027 年，隨著 1.6T Switch 逐步導入 M8/M9 CCL(HLC 層
數 46~52L、玻纖 Low Dk2/Q 布、銅箔 HVLP4/5) ，預期 CCL 廠商產
品規格顯著升級。

三.  台燿高階網路 Switch 板材、低軌衛星板材、1.6T 光
模塊板材，27 年規格朝向高階型號發展 
展望 2H26，在高階 Switch 升級方面，800G Switch 採用 M8、M9 
CCL 規格，2H26 台燿將新增高階 Switch 客戶，隨著 AI 伺服器、
高階網路交換器產業發展朝向 800G、1.6T Switch 升級趨勢帶動之
下， 
銅箔基板需要更高速傳輸、訊號低損耗之下會陸續採用 CCL M8、
M9 規格。此外，2H26 開始 800G 與 1.6T 光纖收發模組(採用 M8、
M9 CCL)開始放量，AI 資料中心帶動光纖收發模組需求，400G 以
上光纖收發模組出貨量 2026 年 1 億個、2027 年 1.45 億個、2028 年
達到 1.6~1.65 億個；在中系系統廠商積極拉貨之下，1.6T 光纖收
發模組 2026/2027/2028 年估量 15~20M/55~60M/85~100M ， 1.6T 光纖
收發模組，於 2 年內呈倍數增長。低軌大型 S 客戶 V3 版本新型火
箭搭載低軌衛星 ， 所採用之銅箔基板升級至 M8 等級 ， 材料損耗要
求降低，訊號傳出完整性標準提高，對於 CCL、PCB(HDI)、玻纖
與銅箔等規格預期同步升級，推升材料成本與 PCB 產值。

四.預估 2026/2027 年稅後 EPS 38.49/66.22 元 
2026 年受惠 AI 伺服器及 ASIC 需求持續強勁的帶動下，台燿為網
通、高階網路交換器銅箔基板重要供貨廠商，將受惠 AI Server 對
於高階銅箔基板強勁的需求，因而，預估台燿 2026 年營收 618.78
億元，+108.01%YoY，毛利率 31.13%，稅後淨利 114.79 億元，稅後
EPS+38.49元 。 隨泰國廠產能釋出 ， 預估台燿 2027年營收增至 963.61
億元，+55.73%YoY，毛利率為 33.26%，稅後淨利 198.23 億元，稅
後 EPS+66.22 元。

四.投資建議維持買進，目標價 2,150 元 
宏遠投顧認為，台燿營運重點在於，營運動能於 2026 年起加速啟
動，一般型伺服器 CPU 廠商 Intel 、AMD 均有新品帶動新平台升級
與相關供應的零組件商機，以及攻入 AI 伺服器、AI 高階網路交換

3 
器的 CCL 、 低軌衛星 CCL 與新增的 1.6T 光纖收發模組規格轉換成
長可期 ， 且台燿在高階的網通 、 網路交換器板材供應 ， 具有一定之

貳、公 司 概 況

一. 公司簡介 
台燿(6274)為銅箔基板廠商，主要產製雙面印刷電路板與多層印刷
電路板之銅箔基板 、 內層銅箔基板 、 黏合片 ， 產品應用別當中 ， ELL
與 SLL(M7、M8)佔比 39%、VLL 與 LL(M4、M6)佔 31%、HDI 與 N-
LL 佔 16%、其他品項佔 14%。

二.2026~2027 年營運概況及展望 
1.HDI 與 M8 以上升級仍為關注焦點，2026~2028 年產能建置積極 
過往一般型伺服器用板採用 HDI 設計，強調細線路設計，CPU 尺
寸變大 Pin Count 數量增加至 2,000 只~4,000 只 Pin Count，PCB 板
面積不變之下要走向細線路化(線寬/線距)朝向更細線路設計。製
程上 HDI 經過多次壓合後，PP 在熱壓多次後，每一層熱膨脹係數
不同，導致通孔 Crack 或翹曲，製作時容易產生爆板，因此對於
PCB 板廠製作良率會是一大技術難關，而金像電、健鼎等廠商於
高階 HDI 相對具備生產優勢與競爭力，相對應台光電、台燿等高
階 CCL 供貨與升級也成為製程一大瓶頸；回顧 3 年來，台燿產能
擴充進程，端視泰國廠進度而定。4Q26 泰國新廠產能陸續開出，
台 燿 總 產 能 為 260 萬 張 /月，+13%YoY。展 望 2027 年，台 燿 泰 國 廠、
常熟廠初步先提升 30 萬張/月，逐步提升總新增產能達 120 萬張/
月 ， 預估 2027 年底時 ， 總產能達 380 萬張/月 ， +46%YoY ； 預估 2028
年底時，台燿總產能擴充至 530 萬張/月，+39.5%YoY，平均 3 年
每年產能擴充約 33%。高階 CCL 在 AI 伺服器、高速網路交換器
(高階 Switch)帶動之下，一旦，泰國廠產能擴充可望使得台燿市佔
率得以提升。2027 年隨著 Nvidia Rubin 平台將帶動 CCL M8+、M9
材料出貨攀升，AI ASIC CCL 拉貨動能呈現 50~60%高度成長，台
光電與台燿等高階 CCL 新產能釋出，產品組合持續優化。

針對 AI GPU 與 ASIC 發展進程，Google TPU 出貨量從 2026 年 400
萬顆上修至 2027 年(V8 增量)增至 800 萬顆，倍數增長。AWS ASIC 
預計 2027 年出貨量達 330~350 萬顆，增長+21%YoY 。NVIDIA 體系
GPU，Rubin 系列層數提升至 24~26L，材料端從 M8/M8+往更高規
格升級，更高層數與多層式架構，促使 PCB 板訊號走線、供電完
整性、高速訊號之阻抗匹配控制、訊號傳輸完整性，皆帶動 PCB

估 3Q26 稅後 12 億元，季增 15%，年增 27%，EPS 7.67 元 
3Q26進入旺季，營收季增 3%至 496億元，年增 48%，估毛利率季減 0.1
百分點至 19.2%，主因低毛利率子公司帆宣比重提高 0.3百分點及記憶體持
續上漲。估稅後 12億元，季增 15%，年增 27%，EPS 7.6 7元，比原估下
修 5%，主因營收低於原先預期 4%。4Q26 NCR代工訂單/帆宣美國二廠持
續增加，估營收季增 3%，毛利率季持平，稅後 14.3 億元，季增 19%，
EPS 9.1元，比原估上修 8%，主因 Kontron持股由 30%上升至 49%。 
提高 Kontron 持股，有助中長期投資效益 
樺漢提高德國子公司 Kontron AG 持股由原先 29.7%上升至 49.4%(8/20交
割完成 )。影響： 1)財務面：本中心 預期提高持股後， 估計可望提升獲利
10%以上。2)軟硬體與通訊互補：Kontron具有嵌入式運算、5G通訊、資安、
交通及國防航太技術，結合樺漢在邊緣 AI 伺服器、機器人及強固型工業電
腦的硬體優勢，從「單點設備銷售」轉型為「平台化解決方案」。 
上修 2026/27 年獲利 1%/6% 
智慧工廠與廠務(帆宣)目前在手訂單 1,578 億元，MoM+17%，未來成長趨
勢明確。展望 2026~27 年主要工程認列包括台積美國 P2~P3及 AP、高雄
廠 P3~P4、新竹 20廠 P3、台中 18廠 P9等，看好新廠商機，包括設備代
工、代理等，其中封裝設備估計 >3 成之成長。由於認列美國二廠業績加快，
估 2026/27年營收年增 55%/14%至 801/913億元，創新高。 
合計樺漢 2026/27 年營收 1,864/2,104 億元，成長 31%/13%，規模經濟向
上，費用率下滑，估營業利益率 5.57%/5.64%，優於 2025年 3.99%。稅後
44.4/53.6 億元，年增 38%/21%，EPS 2 8.25/34.14 元，比先前預期上修
1%/6%，主因升高子公司 Kontron持股。樺漢受惠提升 Kontron持股及帆宣
認列美國二廠增加，2026/27年營收及獲利高成長，維持買進評等。

記憶體漲價大部分轉嫁給客戶，略影響毛利率，但規模經濟向上，營業利益率上揚 
關於記憶體漲價因應，公司已與供應商鎖定全年約 200億元訂單所需之記憶體供貨量，價格雖隨市
場浮動，但會透過長約定價或調價等轉嫁給客戶，若記憶體價格漲幅過大也採客戶 consign 模式，
降低毛利率影響。1Q26 部分客戶接受轉嫁，部分客戶則選擇先觀望遞延專案，造成營收不如預期。
但 2Q26 後客戶已逐漸接受記憶體漲價，出貨恢復正常。由於記憶體轉嫁有落後且難以全數移轉，
加上近期低毛利率的子公司帆宣廠務成長較快，因此估 2026/27 年毛利率 19.4%/19.3%，略低於
2025年 20.5%。但預估 2026/27年整體營收成長 31%/13%，規模經濟明顯向上，費用率下滑，營
業利益率 5.57%/5.64%，遠優於 2025年 3.99%。

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：工業電腦 2026年 10月 19日 Ennoconn 第4 頁，共 19頁  
積極佈局實體 AI 
公司將實體 AI(Physical AI)定義為 AI由數位分析與內容生成，進一步具備感知、理解、決策及實際
執行能力。樺漢過去由 IPC 1.0 工業電腦、IPC 2.0 工業物聯網及 IPC 3.0 智慧平台逐步升級，
2026年開始進一步布局 Physical AI。 
公司建立完整機器人系統工程團隊，涵蓋 AI 感知與理解、機器學習系統、機器人運動控制、嵌入
式運算平台、數位孿生模擬及可靠度設計。 AI 感知包含影像、語音、感測器融合及 VLA（Vision-
Language-Action）技術；模擬驗證採用 NVIDIA Isaac Sim 進行虛擬場景、產線及機器人流程模擬
測試，用以最佳化生產效率。樺漢競爭策略由單純提供板卡與運算模組，轉向提供完整機器人系統
平台。產品布局涵蓋 Sense、Compute、Connect、Control及 Act五大機器人平台零件，應用包括
AMR自主移動機器人、人形機器人、工業製造、智慧物流及商業服務。  
運動控制及機器人相關技術仍在持續投資；模擬驗證軟體毛利率可超過 60%。管理層認為 Physical 
AI 初期仍以研發與平台建置為主，短期商業化重點為工業及商用 AI 應用，人形機器人獲利貢獻仍
需時間。2026年實體 AI為初期發展階段，貢獻樺漢業績約 3億美金，占營收比重 5.2%。長期實
體 AI 於製造場域可協助採購與物流管理、改善長短料問題，並透過即時感測與自動化控制提升工
站品質、精度及生產效率，研調機構預估全球實體 AI產值由 2025年 58億美金，上升至 2035元
產值可達 1200 億美元， CAGR 35.4%。樺漢結合既有智慧工廠與工業物聯網客戶基礎，推動
Physical AI走向實際部署，可望快速成長。

資料來源：公司資料、元大投顧整理 
提高 Kontron 持股，有助中長期投資效益 
因樺漢集團對 Kontron的總持股比例已來到約 29.72%，接近德國證券法規定的 30%強制公開收購
門檻，樺漢董事會正式授權董事長啟動現金公開收購之前置作業(訂定每股 23.5歐元)。樺漢公開收
購並提高德國子公司 Kontron AG 持股由原先 29.7%上升至 49.4%(8/20交割完成)，核心效益與影
響主要體現在以下幾個面向： 
1)財務面：本中心預期提高持股後，估計可望提升獲利 10%以上(少數股權項目扣除減少)，雙方深
化整合後，透過樺漢母公司鴻海製造資源，預期從 2027 年開始全面發酵具體的營收與成本規模經
濟效益。

### Page 6

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：工業電腦 2026年 10月 19日 Ennoconn 第6 頁，共 19頁  
2)業務與技術整合(實體 AI 平台化) 
• 軟硬體與通訊互補：Kontron具有嵌入式運算、5G通訊、資安、交通及國防航太技術，結合樺漢
在邊緣 AI 伺服器、機器人及強固型工業電腦的硬體優勢，從「單點設備銷售」轉型為「平台化解
決方案」。 
• 加速跨場景落地：有助於將無人結帳、智慧零售、 AMR（自主移動機器人）及工廠自動化等應用
標準化，降低客戶導入門檻，並加快在北美及歐洲市場的開案與交付效率。  
Kontron 成長領域包括交通運輸、國防航太、 5G/FRMCS/NAD，以及軟體與解決方案，營收目標
由 2026年 16億歐元上升至 2030年 26億歐元(CAGR 13%)、EBITDA達 4.2億歐元，並藉由提高
平台及服務收入占比改善獲利結構。

結盟 NCR 增加 ODM 訂單 2Q26 開始出貨，有助工業物聯網部門成長 
樺漢與 NCR Voyix (股票代號：VYX US)結盟，該公司是全球消費者技術的領導者，主要生產 POS、
自動櫃員機、條碼閱讀器等，提供高品質的技術解決方案，並幫助企業在快速變化的數位環境中 蓬
勃發展。NCR Voyix 在市場領先的軟體和服務與樺漢的硬體專業知識技術高度互補，並有助樺漢
ESaaS及 ODM業務進一步擴展，為雙贏的結盟。 
NCR年營收約 1,230億元，其中硬體占比 32%，軟體 68%，硬體部分約 400億元台幣訂單未來全
交給樺漢代工，其中 160億元為新接訂單[NCR之前即為樺漢 ODM客戶，60%硬體(約 240億元)
交樺漢生產]，此部分將貢獻在工業物聯網 (含 Kontron、ODM)業務上，2025 年因應川普關稅議題，
NCR IT 全球性服務需調整期，發展整合系統；樺漢墨西哥製造基地調整至田納西。因此原本增加
NCR代工訂單由 10月再度延至 2026年 1月出貨，後因美伊戰爭及 DRAM漲價影響，客戶延至
2Q26正式出貨。受惠此訂單增加，估 2026/27 年工業物聯網營收成長 24%/13%至 845/958億元。

智慧工廠與廠務(帆宣)進入美國二廠密集認列期，營運創新高 
智慧工廠與廠務(帆宣)目前在手訂單 1,578億元，MoM+17%，主要來自晶圓代工廠、記憶體廠擴
廠積極，另外新增資料中心建置，半導體供應鏈普遍面臨交期、產能壓力，加上具有能力承接大型
專案廠商有限，使得廠務接近賣方市場，未來 2~3 年成長趨勢更為明確。展望 2026~27 年主要
工程認列包括台積美國 P2~P3及 AP、高雄廠 P3~P4、新竹 20廠 P3、台中 18廠 P9等，並且看
好新廠商機，包括設備代工、代理等，其中封裝設備估計 >3 成之成長。智慧工廠與廠務(帆宣) 認
列美國二廠業績加快，2026 年前 9 月營收成長 56%至 574 億元，第四季旺季營收持續向上，估
2026/27年營收年增 55%/14%至 801/913億元，創新高。 
2026/27 年年營收及獲利維持高成長 
智慧軟體方案策略進入 IPC 3.0Ⅱ階段，推廣工控/智慧平台，未來專注於雲端+邊緣 AI與大數據外，
亦提升工業級語言模型與機器人技術，聚焦產業包括：國防、航太、交通、零售、建築等。產品包
括：Edge AI Server & Device、Token、AI Agent、Physical AI等，公司積極佈局中，近期因專案
認列延後，估 2026 年營收 218 億元，衰退 3%。2027 年代理/實體 AI 發酵，估營收成長 7%至
233億元。 
合計樺漢 2026/27年營收 1,864/2,104億元(下修 2%/2%，主因智慧軟體方案營收低於預期)，成長
31%/13%，低毛利率的智慧工廠與廠務(帆宣)成長較大及上游原物料 Dram大漲，估 2026/27年毛

產業概況  
工業電腦未來 CAGR 6%，為下游成長穩定的產業 
工業電腦在 2000 年後網路發達，亦造成工業電腦用途日漸擴大，由工廠發展至生活端。過去常受
全球製造業景氣影響，後因應用面變廣，成長性加快。 
工業電腦近年成長率在 5~10%，是下游成長穩定的產業。根據研調機構統計及預估，工業電腦
2023年產值約為 45億美元，可望增加至 2037年的 96億美元，CAGR 6%比一般 PC低個位數成
長為佳，主因公司對工業物聯網、製造業數位化、邊緣 AI 建置…等因素推動，其中半導體、製造
業、汽車、物流、零售…等均為重要成長來源。 
圖 9：工業電腦 2023-2037 年產值 CAGR 6%

資料來源：Markets and Markets、元大投顧整理 
工業 4.0 帶動物聯網需求大增 
從 18世紀末經歷多次工業生產變革，20世紀末進入 21世紀，工業製造由 3.0發展至 4.0，其中最
大改變就是組裝、加工、搬運 …等由原先自動化機台 (工業 3.0)，加入智能回饋，透過物聯網收集
巨量資料，傳輸至雲端運算，統計分析後，即時改善生產製程。隨時因應機台老舊、材料或周遭環
境變化，自動調整量產系統。 
第四次工業革命和過去相比，網路無所不在，移動性提高；感測器體積變得更小、性能更強大、成
本也更低。與此同時，人工智慧和機器學習也開始嶄露鋒芒，相關應用不斷增加。

圖 10：歷次工業變革

資料來源：元大投顧整理 
預估人工智慧物聯網(AIoT)2025~2034 年 CAGR 達 22.16% 
物聯網基礎架構分為五層：物件、通訊與網路、運算與儲存、應用與服務、分析與統計。台灣工業
電腦公司較專注在前端物件 (感測元件)以及中端的運算與儲存二項業務。尤其前端的感測元件為台
灣強項，諸如：POS 機、ATM、機器人手臂、安控攝影機…等。近年受惠人工智慧(AI)導入，成長
加快。 
全球人工智慧物聯網(AIoT)未來受惠於 Data Center、AI Server 建置、企業數位轉型…等因素穩定
成長，研調機構預估 AIoT 2025~2034 年 CAGR 22.16%，為未來工業電腦成長重心。人工智慧物
聯網(AIoT)在全球使用廣泛，目前工業電腦產業使用方面已經陸續切入智慧家庭、車聯網、自動化
設備、機器人…等領域。

維持買進評等，目標價 545 元，潛在上漲空間 34.7%
與樺漢同樣從事工業電腦相關，在國內主要為研華、神基、飛捷、廣積、振樺電等。以 2027 年預
估 EPS來看，樺漢本益比 11.9倍，低於國內同業平均的 13.4倍，顯示樺漢目前股價便宜。
樺漢短期受中東戰爭及記憶體漲價，客戶訂單遞延，1Q26營收表現平淡，但 2Q26結盟 NCR增加

ODM 訂單開始出貨，加上子公司帆宣認列美國二廠進入高峰期，營收快速成長。另外樺漢增加
Kontron 持股，不僅提升獲利，亦有助長期代理/實體 AI 發展，因此維持買進評等。目標本益比同
先前 16倍，以 2027年 EPS 34.14元計算，目標價 545元，潛在上漲空間 34.7%。

公司簡介  
樺漢專精於工業電腦硬體解決方案。終端應用包括 POS機、銀行自動化、互動式資訊服務系統、彩券機及工業自動化。產
品組合方面(2025)，工業物聯網 48%、智慧工廠與廠務 36%、智慧軟體與方案 16%。銷售區域 (2025)北美 26%、歐洲
37%、中國 9%、其他 28%。主要生產交由母公司鴻海代工，另外樺漢在歐洲新設匈牙利自有工廠，取得當地娛樂、金融
零售、工業網安等訂單，維繫 ODM業務成長。

ESG 分析  
分別藉由「在 ESG議題上的曝險」和「個別公司在 ESG議題上的執行力」兩個面向來衡量公司於 ESG上的表現。 
► ESG 總分： 樺漢整體的 ESG風險評級屬於低風險，於 Sustainalytics資料庫所覆蓋的公司中排名亦排行於領先的位置，
在科技硬體行業中的公司中排名略為領先同業。 
► 在 ESG 議題上的曝險： 樺漢的整體曝險屬於中等水準，略高於科技硬體行業的平均水準。公司較需要改善的  ESG 議
題包含公司資安、商業道德、人力資源等。 
► 個別公司在 ESG 議題上的執行力：樺漢已採取若干措施來管理與重大 ESG議題相關的風險，但這些措施尚不足以涵蓋
其所面臨的所有風險。儘管如此，該公司並未捲入具有重大財務影響的風險事件。

買進：根據本中心對該檔個股投資期間絕對或相對報酬率之預測，我們對該股持正面觀點。此一觀點係基於本中心對該股之發展前景、財務表現、利多題
材、評價資訊以及風險概況之分析。建議投資人於投資部位中增持該股。 
持有-超越同業：本中心認為根據目前股價，該檔個股基本面吸引力高於同業。此一觀點係基於本中心對該股發展前景、財務表現、利多題材、評價資訊
以及風險概況之分析。 
持有-落後同業：本中心認為根據目前股價，該檔個股基本面吸引力低於同業。此一觀點係基於本中心對該股發展前景、財務表現、利多題材、評價資訊
以及風險概況之分析。 
賣出：根據本中心對該檔個股投資期間絕對或相對報酬率之預測，我們對該股持負面觀點。此一觀點係基於本中心對該股之發展前景、財務表現、利多題
材、評價資訊以及風險概況之分析。建議投資人於投資部位中減持該股。 
評估中：本中心之預估、評等、目標價尚在評估中，但仍積極追蹤該個股。 
限制評等：為遵循相關法令規章及/或元大之政策，暫不給予評等及目標價。

### Page 1

2026 年 10 月 7 日(水)

1/3 
9 月会合以降に日銀を取り巻く状況が変わった印象  
 経済調査部主席エコノミスト藤代宏一 
（℡：050-5474-6123）

・時事通信は10月６日付で「日銀が『基調的な物価上昇率』について、２％程度に到達したと
判断する方向で検討に入ることが５日、分かった」と報じた。記事内には10月29・30日開催
の金融政策決定会合で取りまとめる展望レポートで、そうした認識が記述されるとしてい
る。

・筆者はこの報道が現実になる可能性が高いと判断している。日銀は予てから基調的物価上昇
率が２％に到達する時期を「2026年度後半から2027年度」と予想してきたので（2026年度後
半にあたる）10月にそうした認識を示さないと、ハト派的な印象を与えてしまうことにな
る。

・既に日銀の政策態度は基調的物価上昇率を２％に「引き上げる」段階から、２％以上に「上
振れさせない」段階に移行している。局面が変化した理由としては、やはり原油高とそれに
対する企業の価格設定行動の積極化が大きいだろう。従来、原油高は企業収益を圧迫するこ
とで、賃上げを阻害し、個人消費の停滞に繋がってきた。そうした環境下で内需は拡大しな
いため、原油高は短期的にインフレ要因となっても、やや長い目でみるとデフレ的な色彩を
帯びていた。もっとも、2026年春先以降の原油高局面では、政府の手厚い支援もあり、企業
・日経平均株価は先行き12 ヶ月72,000 円程度で推移するだろう 
・USD/JPY は先行き12 ヶ月 155 円程度で推移するだろう 
・日銀は政策金利を12 月に 1.5％とした後、27 年末までに2.25％とするだろう 
・FED はFF 金利を、年内は4.0％で据え置くだろう

### Page 2

2/3 
は価格転嫁を積極的に進めた。企業物価指数は４月に前月比＋2.8％と即座に上昇し、前年比
伸び率は８月時点でも＋7.6％と高止まりしている。日銀短観（６月および９月調査）では１
年後の販売価格見通しが急伸した。こうした早期かつ大幅な価格転嫁の進展によって、企業
収益は傷みにくくなっている。このことは、名目賃金と名目消費が伸びやすい構図にあるこ
とを浮き彫りにしている。

・日銀が、基調的物価上昇率が２％に到達したとの認識を示すのであれば、それは利上げペー
スの加速を自ら正当化することになる。さすがに10月の利上げはビハインド・ザ・カーブに
陥ったことを認めたと市場参加者に受け止められる恐れがあるため、見送られると予想する
が、（前回利上げから約３ヶ月が経過する）12月会合では利上げが決定されるだろう。そう
なれば、３～４ヶ月に一度の利上げが標準ペースとなることが市場参加者に広く意識される
だろう。

・利上げを巡っては、経済指標に加えて「政府との距離感」を考慮する必要があり、予想は難
儀であった。しかしながら、ここ数週間で情勢は変化したように感じる。９月25日に片山さ
つき財務相が「官邸とも相談の上、私から初めて申し上げるが、先日の日米首脳会談では、
為替について、トランプ大統領から円安への懸念が示された」と発言したことを皮切りに、
同日には城内経済再生相が「アベノミクス的な意味でのリフレーション政策は、もうそうい
う局面は終わっている」とした。また10月５日に公表された９月30日の経済財政諮問会議の
議事要旨では、民間議員である若田部昌澄氏が「デフレではない状況になりつつある現状で
リフレ政策が終焉を迎えているというのは当然のことだ」との見解を示し、さらに９月の金
融政策決定会合で反対票を投じた佐藤綾野日銀政策委員は６日までに共同通信の単独インタ

### Page 3

本資料は情報提供を目的として作成されたものであり、投資勧誘を目的としたものではありません。作成時点で、第一ライフ資産運用
経済研究所が信ずるに足ると判断した情報に基づき作成していますが、その正確性、完全性に対する責任は負いません。見通しは予告
なく変更されることがあります。また、記載された内容は、第一生命保険ないしはその関連会社の投資方針と常に整合的であるとは限
りません。 
3/3 
ビューに応じて「（政策金利を）段階的に調整していく方針には賛成している」とした。９
月18日会合における「主な意見」で内閣府の出席者（おそらく城内大臣）が利上げに消極的
と読める発言をしていたことと整合しないが、それは9月18日から25日の間に政府の態度が変
化した可能性が指摘できる。

・10月会合までに円安が進む、あるいは原油高等の要因によってＦｅｄが利上げに踏み切る事
態となれば、日銀も追随する可能性があるものの、そうした状況に至らなければ、10月は
「既往の利上げ効果を見極めたい」などとして利上げを見送るだろう。

・なお、これらに鑑みて、筆者は2027年７月の審議委員人事が中立ないしはタカ派的な人選に
なると予想する。高田委員と田村委員に考え方の近い人物に白羽の矢が立ち、リフレ派が送
り込まれることはないと考えている。利上げに積極的な２名の後任人事にリフレ派と目され
る人物が浮上した場合、金融市場では長期金利上昇や円安の反応が予想される。

Source PDF: all report pdf/[000660] SK하이닉스(000660) 3Q26 OP 80.1조원 실적 서프라이즈 예상SK하이닉스(000660) 3Q26 OP 80.1조원 실적 서프라이즈 예상3Q26 OP 80.1조원 실적 서_9ee69886.pdf

### Page 1

SK하이닉스 (000660)

Company Brief 
반도체/디스플레이 
Analyst 김선우 
sunwoo.kim@meritz.co.kr 
RA 우서현 
seohyun.woo@meritz.co.kr 3Q26 OP 80.1조원 실적 서프라이즈 예상  
3Q26 영업이익: 상반기와 다르다. 억제 요인들의 추세 반전 전환 
SK하이닉스의 3Q26 영업이익은 전분기 대비 32% 증가한 80.1조원으로 시장기대
치를 능가할 전망이다. 최근 원화강세 흐름 및 상반기 동사의 상대적 실적개선 속도 
열위에 3Q26 영업이익 추정치는 70조원까지 내려갔다 (컨센서스 74조원). 하지만 
이제 동사 출하량 및 판가의 탄력적 복원세가 경쟁사 보다 빠른 실적 개선을 이끌 전
망이다. 3Q26 DRAM과 NAND 출하량/판가 증감률은 각각 +13%/+22%, 
+4%/+15% 수준으로, 각 영업이익은 63조원/17조원으로 예상된다 (OPM 각각 
84%/69% 추정). 그간의 상대적 실적 열위는 이제 반전된다. 이는 1) 판가 전략에 
있어 중장기적 관점에서 고객 중심 유연성 극대화를 꾀했던 부분의 보상적 되돌림이 
발생하고, 2) 업계에서 제일 높은 HBM 판매 비중으로 인해 판가 상승률에 발목 잡혔
지만, 오히려 HBM4 본격 공급이 시작되며 이익기여가 본격화된다. 아울러 3) 모바
일 핵심고객과의 기성 계약이 산업 전반적 LTA 논의로 재검토되며 공급사에 크게 유
리한 조건으로 변화되어 가기 시작한다. 당사는 1Q27부터 모바일 메모리 판가 급등
을 전망하며 (그림1), 2026E/2027E 영업이익의 경우 264조원/454조원으로 상향조정한
다. 4Q26 내 시장 실적 추정치 상향조정 및 투자자 수급의 구조적 개선을 전망한다.

‘지능기술’ 시대, 수요의 가격 역탄력성 속 중장기 기회창출에 주목 
과거 B2C 사이클과 다르게, 금번 B2B 사이클은 수요의 가격 역탄력성을 보이고 있
다. AGI 도달을 위한 선착순 투자가 핵심이기 때문이다. 빅테크 중 낙오자가 발생하
기 전까지 경쟁적 대규모 투자는 지속된다. 최근 동사의 솔리다임 상장 추진보도에 
일부 우려가 제기되고 있지만, 투자자들은 그 이면의 중장기적 기회에 주목해야 한다. 
동사는 LTA 외에도, 다양한 파트너십을 추진하며 중장기 시장 예측력을 증가시키고 
있다. 솔리다임 상장 과정에서 유입되는 현금은 모회사 ‘SK AI co’의 지분교환 및 JV 
구축 등에 활용되리라 예상된다. 이는 중장기 수요를 ‘창출’시키는 개념이다.

순수한 주주가치 제고 목적 환원 재도출 예상. 적정주가 상향조정 
빠르게 증가한 현금흐름 중 ‘잉여’분은 주주가치 ‘회복’에 우선 배분돼야 함이 마땅하
며, 동사 역시 최근 공시를 통해 주주가치 제고와 주가의 저평가 인식을 드러냈다. 현
재 진행중인 40조원 자사주 매입 프로그램 종료 후 즉각적인 신규 환원 정책 도출이 
예상된다. 2027년 메모리 공급부족은 명확하다. 역사적으로 효율적 시장 내, 주가 조
정은 탄력적 복원으로 귀결되어왔다. 동사에 대한 투자의견 Buy를 유지하며, 적정주
가의 경우 320만원으로 상향한다 (26E/27E 평균 P/B 4.0배 적용). 
 Meritz Research  2026. 10. 12

Document 230: AI的饼越画越大，特斯拉为何仍难打动华尔街？

Source PDF: all report pdf/AI的饼越画越大，特斯拉为何仍难打动华尔街？.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-10-22
Pages: 4
Status: ok

### Page 1

三⼤投⾏罕⻅地形成共识：进展真实存在，但估值已充分定价，甚⾄过于饱满。特斯拉正
⾯临⼀个愈发棘⼿的困境：故事越讲越⼤，估值却越来越难以⽀撑。
摘要：
特斯拉第三季度财报显⽰，公司⾃动驾驶、储能和机器⼈业务均取得阶段性进展，⽑利率亦超
出预期，但居⾼不下的运营⽀出、低于预期的盈利能⼒以及依然遥远的商业化兑现时间表，令
华尔街主流投⾏维持观望⽴场。这家市值 1.6 万亿美元的公司，正⾯临⼀个愈发棘⼿的困境：故
事越讲越⼤，估值却越来越难以⽀撑。
特斯拉第三季度营收 280 亿美元， EBIT 仅 7.18 亿美元，远低于⾼盛和摩根⼠丹利的预期，运营⽀
出超预期是主要拖累。尽管⾮ GAAP 每股摊薄收益 0.48 美元⼩幅超预期，但⾼盛已将 2026 ⾄
2027 年 EPS 预测下调，理由正是持续攀升的运营成本。对于股价⽽⾔，⾼盛维持中性评级及 360
美元⽬标价，较当前股价隐含约 7% 的下⾏空间；摩根⼠丹利同样维持中性评级，⽬标价 400 美
元；摩根⼤通对特斯拉股票标注中性评级。
三⼤投⾏罕⻅地形成共识：进展真实存在，但估值已充分定价，甚⾄过于饱满。

尽管如此，财报中的亮点同样不容忽视。北美 FSD 新⻋搭载率从⼆季度的 55% 升⾄超 75% ，⽆
⼈驾驶 Robotaxi 累计⾥程在短短数周内从 100 万英⾥增⾄ 310 万英⾥；储能业务⽑利率升⾄
33.1% ， Megapack 积压订单创历史纪录。这些数据为特斯拉的⻓期叙事提供了真实⽀撑，也是
多数分析师尚未彻底放弃这只股票的底层逻辑。
⽑利率惊喜，利润叙事受阻
特斯拉三季度整体⽑利率 19.6% ，全⾯超出各⽅预期 —— ⾼盛预测 18.5% ，公司汇总市场⼀致预
期 17.9% ， FactSet ⼝径为 17.5% 。
其中，汽⻋业务⽑利率（含股权激励，剔除碳积分）达 18.1% ，超出⾼盛预测的 16.3% ；储能业
务⽑利率 33.1% ，亦⼤幅超出摩根⼠丹利预测的 26.5% ；服务业务⽑利率 15.9% ，创历史新⾼。
⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP 会员 ⼤师课 ⻅闻历 搜索

### Page 2

然⽽，⽑利率层⾯的超预期被费⽤端的失控所抵消。三季度 EBIT 仅 7.18 亿美元，明显低于⾼盛
预测的 10.43 亿美元和摩根⼠丹利预测的 5.26 亿美元；研发费⽤ 27.61 亿美元，较摩根⼠丹利预
测⾼出约 11% 。管理层明确表⽰，为⽀撑 AI 与能源业务扩张 —— 包括 Optimus 、 Robotaxi 及
Megapack—— 运营⽀出和资本开⽀将继续按季环⽐增⻓。
⾼盛随即下调 2026 ⾄ 2027 年 EPS 预测，分别从 0.82 美元和 1.75 美元下修⾄ 0.67 美元和 1.60 美
元，同时略微上调 2028 年预测⾄ 2.50 美元。
摩根⼤通亦对此持审慎态度。按⽬前股价计算， 2028 年预期市盈率仍⾼达 154 倍，⾃由现⾦流收
益率在 2026 ⾄ 2027 年均为负值，这意味着投资者需要为遥远的未来⽀付极⾼的溢价。
Robotaxi 与 FSD ：⾥程碑在加速，但商业化仍在早期
Robotaxi 是本季度最受市场关注的业务进展。截⾄ 10 ⽉ 18 ⽇，特斯拉完全⽆⼈驾驶的 Robotaxi

累计⾏驶⾥程达 310 万英⾥，较 9 ⽉ 3 ⽇的约 100 万英⾥增⻓超过两倍，周增速约 20% ，超出⾼盛
此前预期的 200 ⾄ 300 万英⾥区间上限。
据⾼盛研究报告， 2026 年 1 ⽉ 22 ⽇特斯拉开始完全⽆监督运营⾄今，平均每约 10 万英⾥发⽣⼀
次事故（⽆论责任归属或严重程度）；相⽐之下， Waymo 在 2025 年 7 ⽉⾄ 2026 年 8 ⽉间平均每 18
万英⾥发⽣⼀次，近期数据已达每 36 万英⾥⼀次。差距依然存在，但特斯拉改善速度不可忽
视。
在地理扩张层⾯，管理层表⽰ Cybercab 已在奥斯汀展开运营，并预计下⽉在达拉斯启动；圣安
东尼奥和杰克逊维尔被列⼊后续扩张城市名单，此前已公布的凤凰城和拉斯维加斯亦在推进
中。
FSD 主动订阅⽤⼾数达 174 万，同⽐增⻓ 67% ；欧盟范围内的全⾯审批最早预计在 2026 年 12
⽉，管理层⽬标是在 2027 年⼀季度前获得欧盟统⼀许可。尽管进展积极，但⾼盛认为来⾃
Waymo 等竞争对⼿的压⼒不容低估，并将⾃动驾驶领域的竞争列为主要下⾏⻛险之⼀。

Optimus ：节奏⽐市场预期保守
⼈形机器⼈ Optimus 是本季度另⼀个被⾼度审视的议题。
⻢斯克在财报电话会上确认 Optimus 已在弗⾥蒙特⼯⼚开始量产，但对产能爬坡的表述相当审慎
——12 个⽉内⽬标产量为 1 万⾄ 2 万台，低于部分市场此前的乐观预期，这批机器⼈将⾸先⽤于
特斯拉内部的 Tesla Academy 进⾏⾃我训练和数据⽣成。⾯向外部客⼾的商业销售⽬标被推⾄
2027 年底，消费者端应⽤则更为遥远。
⻢斯克将 Optimus 的量产过程描述为 "S 曲线 " ，强调需要 " ⼀万件事同时运转正确 " ，速度最慢的零
部件或⼯艺将决定整体爬坡节奏。他点名⼿部（灵巧⼿）是当前最⼤的硬件瓶颈，实现⼈类级
别的灵活性和⻓时间可靠运作仍是挑战。
算⼒层⾯，特斯拉计划于 2027 年 4 ⽉将新⼀代 AI5 芯⽚集成进 Optimus ，该芯⽚算⼒据管理层介
绍约为现有 AI4 芯⽚的 10 倍，将⼤幅提升复杂⾏为能⼒。
据摩根⼤通研究报告，其对整个⼈形机器⼈供应链的⽴场因此趋于谨慎，并对 Unitree 、 Estun 、

UBTech 在未来 1 ⾄ 3 个⽉内⾯临预期修正⻛险保持警惕，。

在柏林、德克萨斯和内华达多地推进相关产能建设。
估值 " 已充分定价 "
尽管业务进展不乏亮点，华尔街主流机构对特斯拉股票的态度依然⾼度⼀致。
⾼盛维持中性评级， 12 个⽉⽬标价 360 美元，基于 150 倍市盈率乘以未来 5 ⾄ 8 个季度 EPS 预测
（含股权激励），较当前股价约有 7% 的下⾏空间。摩根⼠丹利维持中性评级，⽬标价 400 美元，
其估值模型包含 44 美元的核⼼汽⻋业务、 145 美元的⽹络服务、 120 美元的 Tesla Mobility 、 35 美
元的储能，以及折扣后 56 美元的⼈形机器⼈价值分配，已相当充分地反映了⻓期期权价值。
⾼盛明确指出，当前估值 " 已充分定价 " （ valuation is full ），盈利能⼒和⾃由现⾦流在近⾄中期将
持续承压，⽽ EPS 改善的幅度和节奏将受到多个领域竞争压⼒的约束。特斯拉的 12 个⽉股价与

标普 500 指数相⽐，过去⼀年已跑输约 24% ，反映出市场在等待兑现的过程中持续消化溢价。
此外，⾼盛表⽰，特斯拉 CEO 在财报电话会上提及， SpaceX 于 10 ⽉ 20 ⽇的财报电话会上披露，
SpaceX 与特斯拉已分别成⽴由独⽴董事组成的特别委员会，就潜在业务合并进⾏讨论。这⼀披
露为特斯拉股票增添了新的变量，但当前信息有限，各⼤机构均表⽰将持续跟踪，尚未将其纳
⼊估值框架。

Economy 
Business Checkpoint: Looking into the logistics 
15 October 2026

Key takeaways 
• Small and mid-sized businesses entered Q3 in relatively solid financial condition, with profitability growth up across both 
groups, according to Bank of America business account data. Nearly half of estimated tariff payments had been refunded by 
September, though the share of these refunds relative to revenue has peaked. 
• In Bank of America business payments data, logistics spending growth per client was up four times as much for small than mid-
sized firms. Additionally, distribution and inventory transactions growth per client has outpaced spending since the spring of 
2025. 
• Growing transportation bottlenecks are re-emerging ahead of the holiday season. Trucking capacity has tightened as driver 
availability remains constrained, according to BofA Global Research. This has pushed freight rates higher and driven strong 
transportation sector payroll growth, particularly among mid-sized businesses, according to Bank of America payments data.

Business Checkpoint is a new, regular publication from Bank of America Institute. It provides a real-time view of the financial health, opportunities 
and challenges facing small and mid-sized businesses across the US economy, leveraging the depth and breadth of Bank of America's proprietary 
data. By analyzing activity among both small and mid-sized firms, Business Checkpoint offers timely insights into business profitability, hiring, 
investment and spending trends. Such data is not intended to be reflective or indicative of, and should not be relied upon as, the results of operations, 
financial condition or performance of Bank of America. 
Businesses end Q3 in fair shape 
While profitability growth remained positive for both small and mid-sized firms in the third quarter, small firms outpaced their 
mid-sized counterparts for the second consecutive quarter (Exhibit 2). Still, there’s been a notable reversal in the profitability of 
mid-sized businesses, growing this quarter after declining slightly for the past two.  
The continued rollout of tariff refunds is serving as a tailwind to some firms' cash positions. That is particularly true for small 
firms. According to Bank of America account data, by September approximately 47% of estimated International Emergency 
Economic Powers Act-related tariff payments had been refunded to companies with less than $50 million in annual revenue.

According to Bank of America business payments data, the percentage point difference in tariff refunds as a share of revenue 
for firms with less than $50 million in annual revenue spiked in June but dropped to nearly zero in September. At the same time, 
the percentage point difference in tariff payments as a share of expenses began rising again relative to the 2023 average 
(Exhibit 1). This suggests that while tariffs continue to affect companies, the refund program has provided temporary relief for 
some businesses.  
Wholesale trade along with transportation and warehousing posted solid profitability growth across business size 
However, the benefits appear uneven. Such refunds have boosted cash flow, but they have not fully insulated firms from broader 
cost pressures.  
Looking across industries, mid-sized construction and wholesale firms had the strongest profitability growth in September 
(Exhibit 3). Notably, transportation and warehousing, as well as wholesale trade were among the few sectors showing positive 
profitability growth for both small and mid-sized firms last month. 
The persistence of logistics, freight and inventory-related cost pressures may help explain why profitability gains have remained 
relatively concentrated in transportation and wholesale trade. However, these sectors also have a higher exposure to rising gas 
prices within their margins (read more on this in the August Small Business Checkpoint). 
Exhibit 3: For both small and mid-sized businesses, profitability growth was positive in transportation and warehousing as well as wholesale trade 
in September 
Profitability growth by sector and business size in September (YoY%, six-month moving average)

Source: Bank of America internal data  
BANK OF AMERICA INSTITUTE

The cost of maintaining supply chains is rising 
In fact, the prices paid component of the September services reading from the Institute for Supply Management indicates still-
building price pressure for firms (Exhibit 4). This marked the highest reading since July 2022.   
Using Bank of America internal data, we identified payments to logistics and freight companies to assess a key part of supply 
chain costs. In June, logistics and freight payments’ growth per client from small businesses grew nearly 30%, though has 
moderated to 12.7% YoY in September (Exhibit 5). Meanwhile, mid-sized firms’ payments growth in this category was up just 
3.2% YoY in September. 
The divergence suggests logistics costs may be disproportionately affecting smaller firms, who are typically less capable of 
absorbing rising costs than larger firms. Larger companies also often benefit from dedicated logistics contracts and greater 
purchasing power, while smaller ones may be more exposed to spot-market transportation costs and fluctuations in freight 
pricing.

Evidence of more moving parts in companies’ supply chains 
Yet there is increasing evidence that tariffs and geopolitics are adding complexity to small and mid-sized companies’ supply 
chains. 
In Bank of America payments data, average distribution and inventory transaction growth per business client has increased 
steadily since the spring of 2025 despite spending growth in this category falling in September (Exhibit 6). Rising transaction 
growth alongside softer spending growth suggests businesses may be engaging with more suppliers, shipments or inventory 
movements even as overall procurement outlays moderates.  
Furthermore, in May 2026, the Global Supply Chain Pressure Index reached its highest reading since the summer of 2022 and has 
continued to remain elevated into October (Exhibit 7). This further emphasizes that transportation bottlenecks, delays and 
procurement challenges are becoming more common globally. While current readings remain below the extreme levels observed 
during the pandemic, the recent move higher indicates supply chain normalization may be reversing.

The trials of trucking  
As of October 1, BofA Global Research's proprietary bi-weekly Truckload Demand Indicator (see Methodology) remained below 
its historical growth signal (Exhibit 9) and the Truck Capacity Indicator, which reflects shippers' views of available capacity, 
tightened significantly, falling from 39.2 to 28.4 (Exhibit 8). This suggests shippers are seeing fewer trucks available relative to 
demand than in the previous survey. 
According to BofA Global Research, the current trucking upcycle is being driven by constrained supply rather than a significant 
improvement in freight demand. While any positive movement in demand is an important indicator as the industry moves into 
peak shipping season between late September and early December, tighter capacity appears to be the dominant force 
supporting freight rates. 
Exhibit 8: Shippers’ view of available capacity has fallen 
significantly as of October 1, 2026 
BofA Truckload Capacity Indicator by year (bi-weekly)

Source: BofA Global Research  
BANK OF AMERICA INSTITUTE

months after 4.5 months above 
BofA Truckload Demand Indicator (bi-weekly)

Source: BofA Global Research  
BANK OF AMERICA INSTITUTE

The reduction in capacity reflects several factors, including stricter commercial driver's license (CDL) and training requirements, 
increased compliance and enforcement measures, and other regulatory changes that have reduced the pool of qualified drivers. 
This has also likely driven some wage inflation within the industry. According to Bank of America payments data, transportation 
and warehousing payroll growth per client has surged since the start of the year, especially for mid-sized businesses (Exhibit 10).  
Having to pay higher wages suggests labor shortages remain a challenge in the sector. Firms may increasingly need to offer 
higher compensation to attract and retain workers as trucking capacity tightens. In turn, such capacity puts upward pressure on 
freight rates, particularly in the spot market where small businesses may be more impacted.

Logistics payments tend to increase in the second half of the year 
September marks a key run-up to the holiday season (read more on signs of holiday spending in the October Consumer 
Checkpoint), with spending on ocean shipping, containers, and other transportation services steadily accelerating ahead of the 
peak shopping period.  
In fact, over the past two years, the month of November has seen ocean shipping/container spending volumes well above their 
2023-2025 monthly average (Exhibit 11). The seasonal pattern likely reflects inventory restocking and holiday-related shipping 
activity. Ocean shipping volumes typically accelerate during the fall months as businesses prepare for year-end demand. 
Exhibit 11: Ocean shipping/container spending volumes were well above their 2023-2025 monthly average in November 
Logistics and freight average payments by category (monthly, indexed, 100 = monthly 2023-2025 average)

Source: Bank of America internal data  
BANK OF AMERICA INSTITUTE 
Distributors and inventory payments’ growth fell ahead of holiday season 
Changes in logistics and freight spending often provide an early signal of inventory trends, as distributors account for a large 
share of supply chain-related spending (Exhibit 12). 
The sharp moderation in distribution and inventory spending growth per client for both small and mid-sized businesses indicates 
that much of the large-scale inventory accumulation seen earlier in 2025 has already occurred (Exhibit 13). In other words, 
businesses appear to be replenishing inventories for seasonal demand rather than significantly expanding stockpiles as they did 
earlier in the year. 
Overall, businesses seem to be entering the holiday season in relatively stable financial condition, supported by tariff refunds 
and positive profitability growth. However, rising logistics costs, tighter trucking capacity and elevated supply chain pressures 
suggest operating conditions are becoming more challenging.

Methodology 
Selected Bank of America transaction data is used to inform the macroeconomic views expressed in this report and should be considered in the 
context of other economic indicators and publicly available information. In certain instances, the data may provide directional and/or predictive 
value. The data used is not comprehensive; it is based on aggregated and anonymized selections of Bank of America data and may reflect a 
degree of selection bias and limitations on the data available. 
Any Business payments data represents aggregate spend from Small Business and/or Business Banking clients with a deposit account or a 
Small Business credit card. Payroll payments data include channels such as ACH (automated clearing house), bill pay, checks and wire. Bank of 
America per Business client data represents activity spending from active Business clients with a deposit account or a Small Business credit 
card and at least one transaction in each month. Small businesses in this report include business clients within Bank of America and are 
generally defined as under $5mm in annual sales revenue. Mid-sized businesses in this report include business clients within Bank of America 
and are generally defined as $5mm-$50mm in annual sales revenue. 
Unless otherwise stated, data is not adjusted for seasonality, processing days or portfolio changes, and may be subject to periodic revisions.  
Revenue tiers are determined by the combination of following factors: 1) stated revenue on small businesses credit applications, 2) actual 
account inflow into Bank of America Deposit Accounts, and 3) third party revenue estimation.  
The alternative hiring indicator consists of payments from Bank of America business clients to business-focused hiring firms which include both 
direct deposits through Automated Clearing House (ACH) and payments via credit and debit cards. 
Supply chain-related spending is comprised of payments to logistics, freight, distribution, inventory, delivery service, and trucking companies. 
These are identified from merchant names and MCC codes. 
BofA Global Research’s trucking indicators are a bi-weekly diffusion-style survey of shipper demand expectations over the next 0–3 months, 
built from responses from surveyed shippers. 
Data regarding merchants who receive payments are identified and classified by the Merchant Categorization Code (MCC) defined by financial 
services companies. The data are mapped using proprietary methods from the MCCs to the North American Industry Classification System 
(NAICS), which is also used by the Census Bureau, in order to classify spending data by subsector. Spending data may also be classified by other 
proprietary methods not using MCCs.  
Additional information about the methodology used to aggregate the data is available upon request.

10:24 October 08 2026 Share
New Zealand incomes and savings are rising, as is the value of households’ financial
assets. However, the value of housing assets remains flat.
Key takeouts from Stats NZ’s latest update on household incomes, assets and savings (June quarter
2026)

### Page 2

Household incomes
Disposable income levels in the economy grew by 5.2% in the year to June, down from 6.5% in the
year to March

Savings and wealth
Household savings have continued to rise, climbing by $2.5b over the past quarter.

Overall household wealth levels were up 1.9% over the past year. Under the surface, the make-up of
household wealth continues to shift.

Interest costs 
Overall household income levels are continuing to rise. Much of that is due to increases in
entrepreneurial earnings (which is in part due to increased earnings in the agricultural sector). Wage
and salary growth has been more limited but has picked up.
Household savings levels have been rising for three years now, with another solid rise over the past
three months. 
Overall household wealth levels have risen modestly. Although the value of households’ financial
assets is continuing to rise, housing assets have essentially shown no growth since late 2023.
Household spending on interest costs rose just slightly in the June quarter. That’s the first increase in
two years. The easing in average borrowing costs seen over the past few years has come to a close
with the RBNZ’s hiking cycle now in train.
Underpinning the rise in overall income levels have been continued increases in entrepreneurial
earnings, which are up 12% over the past year. That will include stronger earnings in the agricultural
sector, which has been boosted by the strength in commodity export prices. That lift in agricultural
earnings has been reflected in stronger economic confidence and spending in many rural regions.
We expect commodity export earnings will remain firm over the year ahead. 
In contrast, wage and salary earnings are up just 2.7% over the past year. That’s up from 1.8% in the
year to March, but it’s a fairly muted rise, especially given the continued increases in the population
and inflation of 4.1%.
June’s large increase in savings builds on the solid increases seen over the past three years.
The savings rate in the June quarter was 3.7% of disposable incomes, down just slightly from last
quarter.
The value of households’ financial assets has continued to rise and is up 4.6% over the past year. 
However, the value of housing and land assets is down 0.9% over the past year, and it has remained
essentially unchanged since late 2023. We expect housing market conditions will remain soft over
the coming year, limiting increases in overall household wealth. 
Households’ spending on interest costs rose just slightly in the June quarter, climbing by 0.3%.
While that’s a relatively small change, it is the first increase that we’ve seen in two years.

### Page 3

Related articles

Media Contact:
Satish Ranchhod, Senior Economist
P: +64 9 336 5668
M: +64 21 710 852
Over the past few years, many borrowers saw their debt servicing costs fall as they rolled off earlier
high mortgage rates and onto lower ones. That helped to support household spending.
However, the easing in average borrowing cost has now ended. Recent months have seen mortgage
rates pushing higher as the RBNZ has begun to hike the Official Cash Rate, with further hikes
expected over the coming year. 
Looking ahead, we don’t expect a significant increase in average household borrowing costs.
However, we won’t see the same support for spending that we did over the past few years.

Document 318: Irrational Exuberance In S&P 500 FEMO_

Source PDF: all report pdf/Irrational Exuberance In S&P 500 FEMO_.pdf
Report source: researchmail轉寄
Date: 2026-10-22
Pages: 10
Status: ok

### Page 1

From:    Yardeni QuickTakes <yardeni-research@ghost.io>
Subject: Irrational Exuberance In S&P 500 FEMO?
Date:    6 hours ago
Irrational Exuberance In S&P 500 FEMO?

YARDENI QUICKTAKES
Irrational Exuberance In S&P
500 FEMO?
By Ed Yardeni & Elias Griepentrog • 21 Oct 2026
View in browser

### Page 2

Photo by Chris Geirman / Unsplash
Are analysts' earnings expectations for the S&P 500 irrationally
exuberant? So far this year, FEMO (fabulous earnings momentum, as
measured by actual earnings per share) has been soaring, while FOMO
(fear of missing out, as reflected in P/E multiples) has been mostly
retreating. Much of that earnings optimism is concentrated in the S&P 500
Information Technology sector, particularly the Semiconductors industry,

### Page 3

where the AI investment boom has fueled extraordinary demand, pricing
power, and profit margins. But can these conditions persist? Let's take a
closer look:
(1) FEMO overwhelming FOMO. The S&P 500 is up 13.3% ytd, despite a
13.5% contraction in its forward P/E. That's because FEMO has
overwhelmed waning FOMO, with forward earnings jumping 31.0% since
the start of the year (chart).
The divergence is even more striking in Information Technology. The

sector's forward earnings has soared 65.5% ytd, while its forward P/E has
plunged 25.9%. The S&P 500 IT stock price index has risen 22.6% ytd,
powered by FEMO (chart).

### Page 4

Industry analysts remain remarkably bullish on the S&P 500's
fundamentals. Forward revenues is up 15.7% y/y, while forward earnings
is up 39.0% (chart).
(2) Semiconductors driving FEMO. Much of that optimism originates in
the IT sector, where forward revenues and earnings are up 57.2% and
91.4% y/y, respectively (chart).
Digging deeper reveals that the sector's extraordinary FEMO originates
largely in the Semiconductors industry, where forward revenues and

earnings are up 121.7% and 176.0% y/y, respectively (chart).

### Page 5

Indeed, semiconductors now account for a record 55.0% of the IT sector's
forward earnings and 40.9% of its market capitalization (chart).
The growing importance of semiconductors extends to the broader
market. Semiconductors now account for a record 20.4% of the S&P 500’s
forward earnings and 15.8% of its market capitalization (chart).

### Page 6

That makes the industry's earnings trajectory increasingly important for
the entire index. Analysts expect Semiconductor revenues and earnings to
grow 61.3% and 74.0% over the next year, respectively. More remarkably,
they expect earnings growth to average 66.1% annually over the next five
years.
These extraordinary expectations help explain why analysts project IT
sector earnings growth of 44.8% over the next year and 42.0% annually
over the next five years (chart).

In turn, analysts expect S&P 500 earnings to grow 19.0% over the next
year and an average of 26.1% annually over the next five years, the latter
approaching a record high.

### Page 7

(3) Pricing power fueling Semiconductors' FEMO. To determine
whether S&P 500 FEMO shows irrational exuberance, we need to assess
whether Semiconductor FEMO does so. Much of the industry's
extraordinary earnings growth reflects unprecedented profit margin
expansion. The industry’s forward profit margin nearly doubled from the
2023 lows to a record 51.8% during the week of October 20 (chart).
The explanation is straightforward: Demand for chips has surged as

hyperscalers and neocloud providers have raced to build AI infrastructure,
while supply is capacity constrained. The resulting pricing pressures are
clearly evident in the PPI for electronic components & accessories (up
26.8% y/y through September) and the import price index for computer &
electronic product manufacturing (up 12.4% y/y through September)
(chart).

### Page 8

(4) Will Semiconductors' pricing power persist? That's the critical
question for assessing whether Semiconductor FEMO is irrationally
exuberant. Investors already seem skeptical. The Semiconductor
industry's forward P/E has plunged to just 14.2 despite surging earnings
expectations (chart). In other words, unlike the tech bubble of 1999, any
irrational exuberance in FEMO has been mostly offset by depressed
FOMO!
The goods news is that demand for chips should remain strong. The nine

largest cloud service providers are expected to spend $957 billion on
capex this year, up 106% y/y, and more than $1.5 trillion in 2027. But
supply is starting to catch up. Global semiconductor manufacturing
capacity is expected to expand 5% this year and another 6% in 2027,
potentially easing shortages and price pressures.
(5) Irrational earnings exuberance? We suspect there is some irrational
exuberance in Semiconductor FEMO, particularly in analysts' consensus

long-term earnings growth expectations. Today's demand-supply
imbalance supports extraordinary profits, but sustaining current pricing
power and margins over a longer time horizon seems optimistic as
capacity expands. With Semiconductors accounting for more than one-
fifth of S&P 500 forward earnings, that exuberance is reflected in the
broader index.
Nevertheless, FEMO remains robust beyond Semiconductors. Analysts
expect Q3 earnings growth of 30.7% y/y, with positive growth across all 11

sectors (chart).

### Page 9

Our diffusion indexes also show that an increasing share of S&P 500
companies is experiencing rising forward revenues and earnings (chart).
Combined with expectations that semiconductor demand will continue to
outpace supply, at least over the near term, this suggests that FEMO
remains fundamentally rational.

Westpac New Zealand Economics
Stopping for gas
• We estimate that New Zealand consumer
prices rose by 0.7% in the September quarter,
with a moderation in fuel costs balanced
against increases in council rates, building
costs and food prices.
• The annual inflation rate is expected to drop
back to 3.7% (down from 4.1% in the year
to  June).
• Measures of core inflation are expected to
remain firm, with some closely watched
measures expected to rise to the top of the

RBNZ’s target band.
• Our forecast is a little below the RBNZ’s last
published forecast for 3.9% annual inflation.

We expect the September quarter inflation report (out
on 22 October) will show that New Zealand consumer
prices rose 0.7% over the past three months. That would
see the annual inflation rate slipping back to 3.7%, down
from 4.1% in the year to June. However, as we discuss

in our recent forecast update, that’s likely to be just a
temporary reprieve with the recent rise in oil prices set to
push inflation up again over the coming months.
16 Oct 2026 | Satish Ranchhod, Senior Economist | +64 9 336 5668 | +64 21 710 852 | satish.ranchhod@westpac.co.nz
ECONOMIC BULLETIN
CPI preview, September quarter 2026 –
Thursday 22 October, 10:45am.

### Page 2

2
Westpac New Zealand Economics
Key drivers of quarterly inflation.
Underpinning our forecast for 0.7% inflation in the
September quarter are some big price movements in
both directions.
The largest move in prices this quarter has actually been
a sharp fall in fuel costs. Fuel prices aren’t low by any
stretch of the imagination. However, average prices for
both petrol and diesel over the September quarter were
lower than in the June quarter when prices rocketed

higher in the wake of the Middle East conflict – see the
chart below. In terms of specifics, the average price for 91
unleaded was around 6% lower in September than in the
June quarter, while diesel prices were close to 16% lower.
Fuel spending accounts for around 4% of the CPI, and
these moves have taken 0.4ppts off inflation this quarter.

While prices at the pump weren’t as high as they were in
the June quarter, fuel costs are still painfully high. And
that’s been bleeding through into higher prices for other
goods and services. Of note, the September quarter
saw large increases in passenger transport costs, with
international airfares up 14% and domestic airfares up

nearly 8%. Together those prices account for around 2%
of the CPI.
We also expect building costs (10% of the CPI) will rise by
1.6% this quarter following a similarly chunky rise in June.
Again, much of that rise has been related to higher global
oil prices which have been flowing through to higher
operating and material costs. That’s occurred at the same
time as the amount of planned construction work has
been rising.
The September quarter also sees the annual increases

in local council rates and charges for services like water
supply (around 4% of the CPI). We expect these costs will
be up around 7.4% this year, continuing the run of large
increases seen in previous years.
Lastly, the past few months saw a 1.0% rise in food prices
(18% of the CPI), largely as a result of the seasonal rise in
the price for fresh vegetables.
On the downside, we’ve seen continued softness in
housing rents, which are the largest component of

the CPI, accounting for 10% of the basket. With low
population growth and ample supply, rents have
effectively been flat since the start of this year. In annual
terms, rental inflation is the lowest it’s been in more
than two decades, with regions like Wellington seeing
outright falls.

Where could we be surprised?
There are some key areas where we see the risk
of a downside surprise. The first is the price for
communications equipment, like mobile phones. After
a large rise in the June quarter, we could see a sizeable

drop in prices this quarter.
There could also be softness in used car prices, especially
with high fuel prices encouraging a shift away from
combustion engine vehicles.
However, the risks aren’t all to one side. Demand for more
environmentally friendly new cars has picked up, and that
could push up prices for those vehicles. In addition, we’re
watching to see if high fuel costs are spilling over into
other prices as we’re seeing in sectors like construction.

Core inflation around 3%, not easing.
With big swings in fuel costs and concerns that this is
spilling over into a broader lift in inflation pressures,
the RBNZ will be paying close attention to the various
measures of core inflation (core inflation measures
smooth through quarter-to-quarter swings in individual
prices and instead track the underlying trend in inflation).
We expect that core inflation measures will point to
continued firmness in underlying price pressures, with

several closely watched measures likely to take a step
higher this quarter. In terms of specifics, we expect:
• CPI ex-fuel inflation: +3.0% yr (vs +2.9% previously)
• CPI ex-fuel and food: +3.2% yr (vs +2.9% previously)
• CPI ex-fuel, food and household energy: +2.9% yr (vs
+2.5% previously)

### Page 3

3
Westpac New Zealand Economics
We’ll also be watching the distribution of price
changes to see if there’s been a material increase in
the number of prices that are increasing. That could
be an early indication that businesses, even those
with a limited exposure to fuel costs, are starting to
factor in expectations of continued high inflation when
setting prices.
As we’ve noted before, the various measures of core
inflation were not low even before the recent fuel shock.

That means the RBNZ has limited headroom to absorb
upside inflation surprises.
Much of that firmness in core inflation has been due
to increases in domestically oriented non-tradables
prices. We estimate these prices rose by 1.3% over the
September quarter. That would see annual non-tradables
inflation rising to 3.6%, up from 3.4% last quarter.
Non-tradables inflation is a key focus for the RBNZ and
it’s been running at these above-average levels for around

a year now. That’s despite the softness in economic
growth and the labour market. In part, this firmness in
domestic inflation is due to continued large increases in
administered prices, like council rates.
On the imported front, lower fuel costs are expected to
see tradables prices falling by 0.2% over the quarter.
That would result in annual tradables inflation dropping
back to 3.9% from 4.9% previously. Excluding food and
fuel costs, we estimate that tradables prices were up

2.2% over the past year (vs 1.9% in the year to June). That
would be the highest level in three years with the fall in
the NZD boosting import costs.

How do our forecasts compare to the
RBNZ’s assumptions?
The RBNZ’s last published forecast from their September

policy statement assumed that consumer prices would
rise by 0.8%qtr (3.9%yr). That’s slightly higher than we’re
expecting. Most of the difference is because the RBNZ is
expecting higher imported inflation.
A result in line with our forecast would give the RBNZ a
little more breathing room ahead of their upcoming policy
review on 28 October. We’re expecting they will leave the
OCR on hold at this meeting, while markets are pricing in
a roughly 66% chance of a 25bp hike.

However, even if the overall inflation result is close to
forecast, the underlying detail will be important. The
RBNZ will be watching closely for signs that high fuel
prices are spilling over into other prices, especially with
global oil prices taking another step higher recently. With
that in mind, the strength of the various core inflation
measures will be a key focus for the RBNZ.
Furthermore, while annual inflation is expected to
soften in the September quarter, this is likely to be just a

temporary reprieve. With a sharp rise in fuel costs over
the past few weeks, inflation is set to reaccelerate back
over 4% before the end of this year, and it’s set to remain
above the RBNZ’s forecast for most of next year.
September monthly prices.
Our forecast for a 0.7% rise in consumer prices in the
September quarter incorporates information from
Stats NZ’s latest monthly prices update. That update
did reveal some softer than expected food price

movements in September. However, that was balanced
against larger than expected rises in airfares and holiday
accommodation costs.

SUMMARY: AI euphoria meets macro headwinds
Tech remained a major driver of equity market performance in Q3. The
Mag 7 outperformed the broader market while the S&P 500 also

posted gains, lifted by mega-cap and tech stocks.
Still, tech’s path in Q3 was volatile. July began with a reversal of the AI
trade as investors questioned elevated valuations, the sustainability of
AI spending and the outlook for semiconductor demand. That led to a
large sell-off in chip stocks, with large falls in Q2 outperformers SK
Hynix and Samsung contributing to a sharp decline in Korea’s KOSPI.
The Philly SOX experienced its worst monthly performance since

2008.
By August, however, sentiment had improved. The S&P 500 reached
fresh record highs, even as US Treasury yields rose, supported by
stronger cloud earnings and signs of AI monetization. September
proved more challenging as higher oil prices, inflation concerns and
rising bond yields weighed on risk assets, particularly semiconductors.
The Fed also raised its policy rate by 25bp on Sep 16.
Yet, despite these macro headwinds, company guidance continued to

indicate strong AI infrastructure demand, with Micron’s stronger-than-
expected revenue guidance on Sep 30 providing further evidence of
robust demand for memory chips. Memory supply remains tight,
semiconductor capex is accelerating, and the industry’s attention has
increasingly turned to bottlenecks in optical networking and photonics,
as well as concerns around AI regulation and safety. Looking ahead,
tech is likely to remain an important driver of equity returns into Q4,

although elevated valuations and tighter financial conditions leave the
sector sensitive to earnings disappointments. Sources: Deutsche Bank Research, Bloomberg Finance LP. Updated Sep 30. Returns in local currency.
Q3 started with doubts about the AI trade but ended with renewed confidence

1. GLOBAL INDUSTRY BREAKDOWN
Tech hardware, including Lenovo, Advantech, and HP Inc, rebounded in Q3. This was partly driven by accelerating AI

server demand and the ongoing data center build-out, although continuing memory shortages raised costs for
hardware manufacturers. Cloud and server providers, like Oracle, Dell and HPE, also highlighted strong AI-related
demand. Semis and equipment were the weakest performers. Elsewhere, entertainment and telecoms posted modest
declines. Finally, in Q3 investor attention increasingly shifted to AI agents, with Meta’s Muse, Microsoft’s Copilot and

Salesforce’s Agentforce among the prominent offerings.
Source: Deutsche Bank Research, Bloomberg Finance LP. Median returns taken. Updated Sep 30.
Total returns for tech sectors over Q3 vs. YTD returns

### Page 5

Marion Laboure | Camilla Siazon |  Q3 2026 Tech Performance Review |  October 1, 20264
2. SEMICONDUCTORS
Chip stocks were hurt initially by a reversal of the AI trade and then by rising bond yields. The Philly SOX index fell
sharply in Q3, after its worst monthly performance in July (-21%) since 2008. Concerns about higher oil prices and
rising rates continued to weigh on the sector in Aug and Sep. SK Hynix was among the weakest performers, amid an

earnings disappointment relative to expectations and an unwinding of leveraged positions. Its Nasdaq ADRs began
trading in July. Despite these headwinds, the semis outlook remains supported by AI infrastructure demand. Within
semis, potential long-term beneficiaries include suppliers of AI infrastructure, memory including high-bandwidth
memory (HBM), advanced packaging, photonics and semiconductor equipment.
Source: Deutsche Bank Research, Bloomberg Finance LP. Updated Sep 30. Returns in local currency.

Microsoft led the group in Q3 after its Azure growth materially exceeded expectations, with strong Copilot adoption

focus in Sep following the launch of Muse. Amazon’s AWS growth also accelerated, underscoring continued cloud
demand. Tesla underperformed and remains the group’s worst-performing stock YTD.

3.1 MAGNIFICENT 7: Microsoft’s earnings rebound
On July 30, Microsoft rose more than 15%, its strongest daily gain since 2008, following results released the previous

evening. The move added ~$450bn to its market cap, the largest one-day increase on record at the time. For context,
that daily increase is larger than the value of any company in Europe (except ASML).
Source: Deutsche Bank Research, Bloomberg Finance LP. Updated Sep 30.

3.2 MAGNIFICENT 7: Market concentration
According to our equity strategists, from end-July to mid-September, tech-exposed stocks within the S&P 500 rose
14% while the remainder declined 3%. This divergence highlights the concentration of market returns in the tech -

exposed sector. In late July, our strategists anticipated a rotation back into technology, which remains underway.
Source: Deutsche Bank Research, Bloomberg Finance LP. Updated Sep 30.

4. REGIONAL BREAKDOWN
After outperforming in Q2, mainland Chinese tech stocks posted large declines this quarter, hurt by July’s sell-off.
The CSI 300 tech series led the declines. Weakness also extended across Asia more broadly, with the KOSPI, Nikkei 225
and Hang Seng Tech down in Q3, driven by a chip sell-off and concerns over further Fed hikes. European tech equities
were more resilient, with the STOXX Europe 600 Tech Index down slightly lower in Q3 but still well above its year-end

level. Within European tech, Infineon underperformed, while Orange illustrated weakness in telecoms outside the index.

5. CRYPTO, AI & FINTECH
Our thematic indicators also faced macro headwinds in Q3, although the fintech and blockchain ETF shown
recovered during the quarter. Bitcoin also recovered strongly during Q3, trading around $83k in late Sep, despite the

setback when the Senate failed to advance the CLARITY Act on Sep 15.

6. M&A
Q3 saw lower announced deal value than Q2, against a backdrop of uncertainty around the upcoming US midterm
elections, ongoing geopolitical conflict and higher oil prices. Separately, several companies, including Oura and
Holtec, have delayed their IPOs, citing uncertainty. Still, there were some notable deals, including Nvidia’s agreement
to acquire Hugging Face for ~$12.93bn, announced on Sep 3, and AMD’s announced acquisition of World Labs for

$8.2bn on Sep 28.

○ ブラジルでは10 月４日に大統領選挙の第１回投票が実施され、 右派ＰＬのフラビオ ・ ボルソ
ナロ上院議員が47.06％で首位、 現職のルラ大統領が45.13％で２位となった。 いずれも過半
数に届かなかったため、両氏は10 月 25 日の決選投票に進む。事前の世論調査ではルラ氏が
わずかに先行していたが、フラビオ氏は保守層を取り込み、治安対策などを訴えて予想以上
に支持を伸ばした。 一方、 ルラ氏は社会保障政策を通じて一定の支持を維持しているものの、
政権評価は割れており、再選に向けた明確な追い風とはなっていない。他候補の得票率はい
ずれも２％台にとどまり、決選投票では中道・右派層をどちらが取り込むかが焦点となる。 
○ 今後は、財政政策や治安対策が主要な争点となる見通しである。ルラ氏が追加的な家計支援
策を打ち出せば財政悪化への懸念が強まる可能性がある一方、フラビオ氏は歳出抑制や財政
規律強化を掲げており、市場がこれを好感する可能性がある。ただし、フラビオ氏は行政府
を率いた経験がなく、政策運営には不透明感も残る。また、政権交代となれば、米国との関
係を重視する方向へ外交姿勢が変化する可能性もある。決選投票は接戦が予想され、その結
果はブラジルの財政・金融政策、 金融市場、 外交政策の方向性を左右する重要な節目となる。

【ブラジル大統領選挙は決選投票へ､フラビオ・ボルソナロ氏が予想以上の得票】 
ブラジルでは10 月４日、 大統領選挙の第１回投票が実施された。 今回の選挙では、 左派ＰＴ （労
働者党） から出馬した現職のルラ大統領が再選を目指す一方、ボルソナロ前大統領の長男で右派Ｐ
Ｌ（自由党）から出馬したフラビオ・ボルソナロ上院議員が政権奪還を目指して立候補し、両氏を
軸とする争いとなった。開票率 99.90％段階では、フラビオ氏の得票率が 47.06％と首位に立つ一
方、ルラ氏は 45.13％で２位となっている。いずれの候補も当選に必要な有効票の過半数には届か
ず、両氏は10 月25 日に予定される決選投票に進むことになる。 
今回の結果で注目されるのは、フラビオ氏が事前の世論調査を上回る得票を確保したことである。
投票前日に公表された世論調査では、有効票ベースでルラ氏が 45％、フラビオ氏が 42％となり、
両氏は統計上の誤差の範囲内ながら数字のうえではルラ氏が先行していた。 ９月下旬の調査におい
ても、ルラ氏が先行する結果が多くみられたことを勘案すれば、 第１回投票ではフラビオ氏が選挙

### Page 2

2/3 
戦の最終盤にかけて支持を上積みした格好となる。 
背景のひとつには、 ボルソナロ前大統領を支持してきた保守層をフラビオ氏が取り込んだことが
ある。今回の大統領選挙では、経済や治安などが主要な争点となるなか、フラビオ氏は犯罪対策の
強化などを前面に押し出した。一方、ルラ政権は、低所得者層向けの現金給付策（ボルサ・ファミ
リア）をはじめとする社会保障政策を通じて一定の支持を維持している。 さらに、10 月からは給付
額が約 15％引き上げられるなど、 大統領選を意識した動きもみられた。 しかし、 政権に対する評価
は割れている。 投票直前の世論調査では、 ルラ政権を 「悪い ・ 非常に悪い」 と評価する割合が41％、
「良い・非常に良い」 とする割合が35％となり、 政権への評価が再選への追い風になっていない様
子が示されていた。 
大統領選には計 12 人が立候補したものの、フラビオ、ルラ両氏以外の候補への支持は限定的と
なった。 投票前には、中道・アバンテから出馬した精神科医で作家のアウグスト・クリー氏、極右 ・
ミッション党から出馬したレナン・サントス氏、中道右派・社会民主党から出馬した元ゴイアス州
知事のロナウド・カイアド氏が一定の支持を集めていた。しかし、これらの候補者の得票率はいず
れも２％台にとどまり、２候補との差は大きく開いた。このため、決選投票では、これらの候補に
投票した有権者、とりわけ中道・右派層をどちらが取り込むことができるかが焦点となる。投票直
前の世論調査における決選投票の想定では、ルラ氏 47％、フラビオ氏 46％とほぼ拮抗しており、
選挙戦は第１回投票以上に接戦となる可能性がある。

【決選投票に向けて、金融市場では経済政策の行方に注目が集まると予想される】 
決選投票に向けては、経済政策も重要な争点となる。ルラ政権は、社会保障の拡充や所得再分配
政策を重視する一方、金融市場には財政支出の拡大に伴う財政悪化への懸念がくすぶっていた。 そ
のため、 ルラ氏が再選に向けて追加的な家計支援策などを打ち出す場合には、 財政運営に対する市
場の懸念が一段と高まる可能性がある。
一方のフラビオ氏は、歳出抑制や財政規
律強化を通じ て公的債務の抑制に取り
組む方針を掲げている。 大統領選と同時
に実施された議会選挙においても、 中道
右派や右派勢力が勢力を拡大しており、
仮にフラビオ氏が勝利すれば、 金融市場
がこうした姿勢を好感する可能性はあ
る （図１） 。 とはいえ、 フラビオ氏はリオ
デジャネイロ州議を４期、 上院議員を１
期務めるなど政治経験は長いものの、行
政府を率いた経験はなく、具体的な政策運営や議会との関係構築などを巡って不透明な部分は少な
くない。足元のブラジル経済は、 インフレ圧力を背景とする高金利が内需の重石となっているうえ、
財政運営への懸念が高金利の一因となってきた。 こうした状況を勘案すれば、選挙後の財政運営に

### Page 3

経済研究所が信ずるに足ると判断した情報に基づき作成していますが、その正確性、完全性に対する責任は負いません。見通しは予告
なく変更されることがあります。また、記載された内容は、第一生命保険ないしはその関連会社の投資方針と常に整合的であるとは限
りません。
3/3
対する市場の評価は、 金融環境や中銀の政策運営にも影響を与えると予想される。決選投票に向け
て、両候補がいかなる財政政策の方向性を示すかに市場の関心が集まるであろう。
さらに、選挙結果はブラジルの外交政策にも影響を与えるとみられる。ルラ政権はこれまで、
BRICS をはじめとする新興国との関係強化を図るとともに、米中双方と一定の距離を取りつつ独自
外交を展開してきた。一方、フラビオ氏はボルソナロ前政権の政治的な流れを引き継ぎ、トランプ
米政権との関係を重視する姿勢を示している。このため、政権交代となれば、対米関係や対中関係
を含む外交姿勢にも変化が生じる可能性がある。
10 月25 日の決選投票に向けては、 第１回投票で首位となったフラビオ氏が勢いを維持できるか、

それともルラ氏が巻き返すかが焦点となる。 第１回投票の結果は、 事前の世論調査に比べてフラビ
オ氏への支持の強さを示すものとなったものの、 決選投票を想定した直近の世論調査では両氏の支
持率は拮抗している。このため、今後３週間の選挙戦では、他候補の支持層の取り込みに加え、財
政政策や治安対策などを巡る両候補の主張が有権者の判断を左右するとみられる。 大統領選挙の行
方は、ブラジルの政治のみならず、財政・金融政策や金融市場、さらには外交政策の方向性を占う
うえでも重要になろう。
以 上
